# Native authorization findings and source comparisons

- **Status:** active
- **Owning queue item:** [ADOPT-002](active-work.md#adopt-002)
- **Completion / archival evidence:** source navigation prepared for B01 through B16; later service boundaries pending

The normal reviewer ledger has **20 open findings: six HIGH and fourteen MEDIUM**. All remain undecided. This file records locations and reading commands; it does not accept or dismiss a finding, establish semantic equivalence, or record independent review or operator security acceptance. Earlier counts of seven omitted older ledger entries.

- Original path prefix: `components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/`.
- Generated path prefix: `_build/native-review-source/`. The file and one-based line references below refer to the materialized source, not the forwarding bindings.
- The normal materializer checks the manifest, authority pins, cache entries, file hashes and all 1,207 existing acceptance batches for all 56 libraries. Materialized source bytes match every manifest file digest. No new generation or qualification was performed for this guide.
- Reviewed ledger SHA-256: `5bcb416b227c64af9f74a4e3f4c58e020178bfd42122020b98ef7a470caac224`; zero overrides. Read it with `liminal-review log --repo .`. Do not run an accept or dismiss command.

## Read original and generated source side by side

From the repository root, materialize the exact accepted source into an unused directory:

```sh
node --input-type=module <<'JS'
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import {prepareNative} from "./components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/scripts/native-libraries.mjs";
const implementation = path.resolve("components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation");
const destination = path.resolve("_build/native-review-source");
if (fs.existsSync(destination)) throw new Error("Choose an unused review directory, or verify the existing source against the manifest before reading it");
const {libraries} = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(implementation, "native/libraries.json"), "utf8"));
console.log(prepareNative(implementation, [destination], {components: libraries.map(item => item.component)}));
JS
```

Then define this shell helper. Every `pair` command below supplies exact original and generated ranges. A `diff` status of 1 means the text differs and is expected. Read both branches and their callers; textual comparison is not an authorization verdict.

```sh
MAIN=ba43c5b28cc0620472b4a09ab699fbc529c1afcb
ORIGINAL_PREFIX=components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation
GENERATED_ROOT=_build/native-review-source
pair() {
  diff -y -W 240 \
    <(git show "$MAIN:$ORIGINAL_PREFIX/$1" | nl -ba | sed -n "$2,${3}p") \
    <(nl -ba "$GENERATED_ROOT/$4" | sed -n "$5,${6}p")
}
```

### Constant validation patterns

The returned path-ID and session-key patterns are defined at original `lib/v2/validate.ts:137` through 160 and materialized `field_validation/create_validators.js:8` through 11. Read both definitions with `pair lib/v2/validate.ts 137 160 field_validation/create_validators.js 8 11`. The b64url and list operations are shown separately above.

## Complete operation and guard-site catalog

This catalog includes the remaining admission, authorization, validation and limit operations, plus adjacent conditional behavior needed to inspect them. Within each original operation, every indexed conditional, throw, policy/validation call and security option is listed by its exact line. The generated column lists all guard sites in the selected comparison operation. Split or inlined operations use the full target module. Reading scopes are deliberately broader than a single guard so factored checks and their call sites remain visible. The grouped sites are not a claim of one-to-one syntax or semantic equivalence.

Use `pair` with each row's original and generated range. Main and generated location prefixes are defined above. A group with multiple target operations has a separate command for each. Original helper modules introduced on this branch are not invented as main paths: their original operations are indexed in `lib/config.ts`, `lib/v2/config.ts`, `lib/v2/core.mts`, `lib/v2/db.ts`, `lib/secret.ts`, and the original identity modules. Main's original spend parsers live in its `spend/` directory, not in the new public binding files.

## Schema and input-data constraints

Read the original SQL constraints as well as executable guards. `pair lib/v2/db.ts 120 1105 storage_schema/change_programs.js 1 34` starts the comparison; read the complete materialized `change_programs.js` for every migration string. It contains the declared schema program data, including constraints, foreign keys, indexes and migration statements. The runtime migration and transaction pairs above show where those programs are applied. The original source function and generated constant may have different physical wrapping, so a single source line can contain several SQL statements.

The B13 disposable-domain table is an immutable input, not a generated security primitive. Compare the B23 asset digest and the `isDisposable` / `refuseDisposable` operations. Cryptographic and provider libraries remain caller-supplied resources; the generated signature, token and policy call sites are the locations for reviewing their use.

## Security-relevant retained adapters

The approved supporting classification retains these checks in their original target adapters. They have no relocated generated counterpart. Main means the pinned revision above; current means source checkpoint `c4b853866ff5e130fa294f8e70fe5c0530e87aa4`, whose runtime bytes are unchanged. Both sets are readable with `git show REV:components/legacy-project-wrapper/implementation/PATH | nl -ba`. The exact 280-path membership and hashes are recorded in the approved retention list.

## Review state

The authoritative ledger remains the source of finding status. All twenty IDs above are open, with zero overrides and no accept/dismiss action. Passing functional tests and providing readable generated source do not decide those findings. Independent review and actual operator security acceptance belong to the operator.

The operation catalog includes 522 original operation groups and 2381 guard, call and option sites. This count describes navigation coverage, not a count of security findings or independently accepted checks.
